0001  000000  %#MODEL nd100
0002  000000  %
0003  000000  % ND-100 MICROCODE, 3033E-CX CARD: BASE BANK (PROM SET 015XX) AND CX OPTION (PROM SET 085XX)
0004  000000  %
0005  000000  % Reconstructed source. The base bank (0 - 3777) is the program of the printed listing "48-BITS
0006  000000  % ND-100 (PROM 015XX)" (scan ND100-MICROCODE.pdf, pages 90-218); the extension bank (4000 and
0007  000000  % up) is the printed listing "CX-OPTION ND-100 (PROM 085XX)" (pages 236-311). Labels, comments
0008  000000  % and operand names are the ones of the print. The instruction words are the ones of the PROM
0009  000000  % chips of the 3033E-CX card: this file assembles with "ndmicroasm nd100-asm" to exactly the
0010  000000  % two chip images 3033E-CX-microcode.CODE and 3033E-CX-microcode_ext.CODE put together (35370
0011  000000  % bytes, see REFERENCE/README.md). Where the print and the chips differ, the chips win:
0012  000000  %   - CYCL: the print gives CYCL,FAST, CYCL,NORM or CYCL,WAIT or no CYCL at all (no CYCL is
0013  000000  %     the same as CYCL,FAST, bits 21-20 = 0). Where a word has no CYCL in the print, none is
0014  000000  %     written here if the chips hold FAST; every other word has the cycle of the chips.
0015  000000  %   - 003514-003517: the print jumps to 5INIT, ENTR, LEAV and ELEAV (base bank copies); the
0016  000000  %     PROMs jump to ILLIN.
0017  000000  %   - Words that are not in the print are written from the chip decoding without comment.
0018  000000  % The printed base listing and the printed CX listing are two programs that were assembled
0019  000000  % apart (the CX listing reaches the base labels through the old label list on pages 84-87).
0020  000000  % Nine names are in both: 5INIT COBVC COMME ELEAV ENTR ENTRB ENTRC LEAV TSTAC. One assembly
0021  000000  % cannot hold a name twice, so the copy in the extension bank (address 4000 and up) is written
0022  000000  % with the suffix _CX (for example ENTR_CX at 6652).
0023  000000  %
0024  000000  %********************************************************************
0025  000000  % 48-BIT N-VERSION
0026  000000  %********************************************************************
0027  000000  % ADDRESS 0 ENTERED AT :
0028  000000  % MASTER CLEAR
0029  000000  % PAGE FAULT
0030  000000  % PROTECT VIOLATION
0031  000000  % ROM OUT OF RANGE
0032  000000  %********************************************************************
0033  000000  0/
0034  000000            A,17 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
0035  000000            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0036  000000            COND,FETCH F,NEXT F,HOLD;
0037  000001            A,15 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
0038  000001            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD
0039  000001            FTCH CONDENABL;
0040  000002  %********************************************************************
0041  000002  % INCREMENT P IF NOT FETCH
0042  000002            B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0043  000002            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
0044  000003  FTCH:     A,13 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
0045  000003            IDBS,BMG COMM,CLIRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD
0046  000003            COND,IRQ F,JMP F,HOLD;
0047  000004            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
0048  000004            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0049  000004            NOINT CONDENABL;
0050  000005  %********************************************************************
0051  000005  % INTERRUPT IS PENDING
0052  000005            ALUD,NONE
0053  000005            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
0054  000005            COND,STP F,JMP F,HOLD;
0055  000006            ALUD,NONE
0056  000006            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
0057  000006            FTCH2 CONDENABL;
0058  000007  %********************************************************************
0059  000007  % CPU IS IN STOP MODE, MULTIPLE SINGLE OR BREAKPOINT MUST BE THE CASE
0060  000007            AB,BPFLG ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
0061  000007            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0062  000007            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0063  000010            AB,SINGL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
0064  000010            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
0065  000010            BRKZZ CONDENABL;
0066  000011            ALUD,NONE
0067  000011            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
0068  000011            BRKZZ CONDENABL;
0069  000012  %********************************************************************
0070  000012  % NO INTERRUPT IS PENDING, IT CAN BE MASTER CLEAR OR IOF-PAGEFAULT
0071  000012  NOINT:    ALUD,NONE
0072  000012            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
0073  000012            COND,FETCH F,NEXT F,HOLD;
0074  000013            ALUD,NONE
0075  000013            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
0076  000013            FTCH2 CONDENABL;
0077  000014  %********************************************************************
0078  000014  % DECREMENT P, IT HAS BEEN INCREMENTED
0079  000014            B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
0080  000014            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
0081  000014            FTCH2;
0082  000015  %********************************************************************
0083  000015  % EXECUTE LEVEL CHANGE OR JMP *
0084  000015  FTCH2:    PIC,RVECT ALUD,NONE
0085  000015            IDBS,GPR T,MAPJ T,JMP T,HOLD
0086  000015            FETCH;
0087  000016  %********************************************************************
0088  000016  %********************************************************************
0089  000016  % MAPPING POINT FOR ALL INSTRUCTIONS. NEXT MICROADDRESS IS TAKEN FROM
0090  000016  % MAPPING PROM, OR CONTROLLED BY THE INTERRUPT HARDWARE (INTERRUPT VECTOR)
0091  000016  %********************************************************************
0092  000016  FETCH:    A,P B,Z ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B,YA
0093  000016            IDBS,ALU MIS,FETCH COMM,RDRQ T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,FAST;
0094  000017  %********************************************************************
0095  000017  % RETURN FROM P-MODIFYING ROP- OR BOP-INSTRUCTION
0096  000017  PMODX:    ALUD,NONE
0097  000017            IDBS,DSABL T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
0098  000020  %********************************************************************
0099  000020  % RETURN AFTER JUMPS, OR WHENEVER NEW FETCH OF INSTRUCTION HAS TO BE DONE
0100  000020  INIT:     B,P ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
0101  000020            IDBS,ALU MIS,FETCH COMM,RDRQ T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,FAST;
0102  000021  %********************************************************************
0103  000021  % RETURN WITH NEXT INSTRUCTION IN DBR
0104  000021  USDBR:    PIC,RVECT B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0105  000021            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,MAPJ T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0106  000021            FETCH;
0107  000022  %********************************************************************
0108  000022  % RETURN AFTER STORE-INSTRUCTIONS, WITH NEXT INSTRUCTION IN GPR
0109  000022  STEND:    A,P B,R1 ALUF,A-B ALUD,NONE
0110  000022            IDBS,ALU COMM,ESRP T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0111  000022            COND,R=P F,JMP F,HOLD;
0112  000023            PIC,RVECT B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0113  000023            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,MAPJ T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0114  000023            FETCH CONDENABL;
0115  000024  %********************************************************************
0116  000024  % *+1 HAS BEEN CHANGED
0117  000024            PIC,LOSTS B,P ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
0118  000024            IDBS,AARG COMM,EPIC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0119  000024            10;
0120  000025            ALUD,NONE
0121  000025            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0122  000025            INIT;
0123  000026  %********************************************************************
0124  000026  % RETURN WITH NEXT INSTRUCTION IN GPR
0125  000026  USGPR:    PIC,RVECT B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0126  000026            IDBS,GPR T,MAPJ T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0127  000026            FETCH;
0128  000027  %********************************************************************
0129  000027  % CODE FOR ILLEGAL INTRUCTION
0130  000027  ILLIN:    A,7 ALUD,NONE
0131  000027            IDBS,BMG COMM,SMPID T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0132  000027            INIT;
0133  000030  %********************************************************************
0134  000030  % CODE FOR PRIVILEGED INSTRUCTION IF ON RING 0-1
0135  000030  PRIVI:    A,11 ALUD,NONE
0136  000030            IDBS,BMG COMM,SMPID T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0137  000030            INIT;
0138  000031  %********************************************************************
0139  000031  %********************************************************************
0140  000031  %
0141  000031  % STORE INSTRUCTIONS
0142  000031  %
0143  000031  %********************************************************************
0144  000031  STZ:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0145  000031            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0146  000031            MREFS;
0147  000032            ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
0148  000032            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0149  000032            STEND;
0150  000033  STA:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0151  000033            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0152  000033            MREFS;
0153  000034            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0154  000034            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0155  000034            STEND;
0156  000035  STT:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0157  000035            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0158  000035            MREFS;
0159  000036            A,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0160  000036            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0161  000036            STEND;
0162  000037  STX:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0163  000037            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0164  000037            MREFS;
0165  000040            A,X ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0166  000040            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0167  000040            STEND;
0168  000041  STD:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0169  000041            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0170  000041            MREFS;
0171  000042            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0172  000042            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0173  000042            WRRQ;
0174  000043            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0175  000043            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0176  000043            STEND;
0177  000044  STF:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0178  000044            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0179  000044            MREFS;
0180  000045            A,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0181  000045            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0182  000045            WRRQ;
0183  000046            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0184  000046            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0185  000046            WRRQ;
0186  000047            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0187  000047            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0188  000047            STEND;
0189  000050  MIN:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0190  000050            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0191  000050            MREFL;
0192  000051            B,R2 ALUF,D+1 ALUD,B
0193  000051            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0194  000051            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0195  000052            B,R1 ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
0196  000052            IDBS,ALU COMM,WRRQ MIS,HOLD T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0197  000052            COND,COND F,JMP F,POP;
0198  000053            A,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0199  000053            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0200  000053            STEND CONDENABL;
0201  000054            B,P ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0202  000054            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0203  000054            INIT;
0204  000055  %********************************************************************
0205  000055  %********************************************************************
0206  000055  % LOAD INSTRUCTIONS
0207  000055  %
0208  000055  %********************************************************************
0209  000055  LDA:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0210  000055            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0211  000055            MREFL;
0212  000056            B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0213  000056            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0214  000056            USGPR;
0215  000057  LDT:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0216  000057            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0217  000057            MREFL;
0218  000060            B,T ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0219  000060            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0220  000060            USGPR;
0221  000061  LDX:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0222  000061            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0223  000061            MREFL;
0224  000062            B,X ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0225  000062            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0226  000062            USGPR;
0227  000063  LDD:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0228  000063            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0229  000063            MREFL;
0230  000064  LDAD:     B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0231  000064            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
0232  000064            RDRQ;
0233  000065            B,D ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0234  000065            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0235  000065            USGPR;
0236  000066  LDF:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0237  000066            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0238  000066            MREFL;
0239  000067            B,T ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
0240  000067            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
0241  000070  NEXT:     B,R1 ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0242  000070            IDBS,ALU MIS,HOLD COMM,RDRQ T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0243  000070            LDAD;
0244  000071  ADD:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0245  000071            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0246  000071            MREFL;
0247  000072            A,A B,A ALUF,D+A ALUD,B STS,EA
0248  000072            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0249  000072            USGPR;
0250  000073  SUB:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0251  000073            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0252  000073            MREFL;
0253  000074            A,A B,A ALUF,A-D ALUD,B STS,EA
0254  000074            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0255  000074            USGPR;
0256  000075  AND:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0257  000075            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0258  000075            MREFL;
0259  000076            A,A B,A ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
0260  000076            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0261  000076            USGPR;
0262  000077  ORA:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0263  000077            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0264  000077            MREFL;
0265  000100            A,A B,A ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B
0266  000100            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,WAIT
0267  000100            USGPR;
0268  000101  %********************************************************************
0269  000101  %********************************************************************
0270  000101  % MULTIPLY SINGLE PRECISION
0271  000101  %********************************************************************
0272  000101  MPY:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0273  000101            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
0274  000101            MREFL;
0275  000102            B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % SAVE GPR (NEXT INSTR)
0276  000102            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
0277  000103            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q  % READ STATUS
0278  000103            IDBS,STS T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
0279  000104            B,STS ALUF,MASKDQ ALUD,B  % RESET DYN. OVERFLOW
0280  000104            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0281  000104            20;
0282  000105            A,A B,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B  % COPY A-REG.
0283  000105            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0284  000105            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0285  000106            B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % READ MEM. OPERAND
0286  000106            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0287  000106            MPY1 CONDENABL;
0288  000107            B,R2 ALUF,-B ALUD,B  % NEGATIVE A-REG.
0289  000107            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0290  000107            COND,COND F,JMP;
0291  000110  MPY1:     B,16 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,Q  % LOAD LOOP-COUNTER
0292  000110            IDBS,BARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0293  000110            MPY2 CONDENABL;
0294  000111            A,R3 ALUF,-A ALUD,NONE  % NEG. MEM.-OPERAND
0295  000111            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
0296  000112  MPY2:     B,R5 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
0297  000112            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
0298  000112            COND,LC=0;
0299  000113            A,R2 B,R5 MIS,ZIN ALUF,A+B ALUM,FMU ALUD,SRD  % MAKE RESULT IN R5
0300  000113            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT
0301  000113            LCOUNT;
0302  000114            B,R5 ALUF,PASSB ALUD,SLD
0303  000114            MIS,APT T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
0304  000115            B,R5 MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
0305  000115            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
0306  000115            COND,F=0 F,NEXT;
0307  000116            B,R4 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
0308  000116            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0309  000116            MPY3 CONDENABL;
0310  000117            B,R4 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % SET DYN. & STAT. OVF.
0311  000117            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0312  000117            60;
0313  000120  MPY3:     A,STS B,R4 ALUF,ORAB ALUD,NONE STS,LO
0314  000120            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
0315  000121            A,A B,R3 ALUF,XORAB ALUD,NONE  % TEST SIGN
0316  000121            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0317  000121            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0318  000122            B,A ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
0319  000122            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0320  000122            MPY4 CONDENABL;
0321  000123            B,A ALUF,-B ALUD,B  % INVERT RESULT
0322  000123            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
0323  000124  MPY4:     A,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE  % FILL GPR WITH NEXT
0324  000124            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,POP CYCL,FAST
0325  000124            USGPR;
0326  000125  %********************************************************************
0327  000125  %********************************************************************
0328  000125  % JUMP INSTRUCTIONS
0329  000125  %********************************************************************
0330  000125  JMP:      ALUF,D-1
0331  000125            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
0332  000125            MREFJ;
0333  000126  JPL:      A,P B,L ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
0334  000126            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
0335  000126            JMP;
0336  000127  JAZ:      A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0337  000127            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP
0338  000127            COND,F=0 F,RETURN F,HOLD;
0339  000130            B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0340  000130            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP T,HOLD
0341  000130            CJMP CONDENABL;
0342  000131  %********************************************************************
0343  000131  % CONDITIONAL JUMP IS TRUE
0344  000131  CJMP:     A,R1 B,P ALUF,A+B ALUD,B
0345  000131            T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
0346  000131            INIT;
0347  000132  JAF:      A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0348  000132            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP
0349  000132            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0350  000133            B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0351  000133            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,RETURN T,HOLD
0352  000133            CJMP CONDENABL;
0353  000134  JAN:      A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE

0355  000134            COND,F15 F,RETURN F,HOLD;
0356  000135            B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B

0362  000137            B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0363  000137            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,RETURN T,HOLD
0364  000137            CJMP CONDENABL;
0365  000140  JXZ:      A,X ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
0366  000140            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP
0367  000140            COND,F=0 F,RETURN F,HOLD;
0368  000141            B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0369  000141            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP T,HOLD
0370  000141            CJMP CONDENABL;
0371  000142  JPC:      B,X ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
0372  000142            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP CYCL,FAST
0373  000142            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
0374  000143            B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
0375  000143            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,RETURN T,HOLD

0379  000144            COND,F15 F,RETURN F,HOLD;

0827  000352            ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,Q

1167  000521            A,17 B,R2 ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,B
1168  000521            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1169  000521            FAD2;
1170  000522  %********************************************************************
1171  000522  %********************************************************************
1172  000522  % FLOATING ADD ROUTINE
1173  000522  %********************************************************************
1174  000522  FAD:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1175  000522            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH
1176  000522            MREFL;
1177  000523            B,R2 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1178  000523            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1179  000524  %********************************************************************
1180  000524  % COMMON PART OF FAD & FSB
1181  000524  FAD2:     B,STS ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1182  000524            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1183  000524            2;
1184  000525            A,5 B,R6 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1185  000525            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD;
1186  000526            A,17 B,R5 ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1187  000526            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1188  000527            A,R5 B,T ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,Q
1189  000527            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1190  000530            A,R2 B,R5 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,B
1191  000530            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1192  000530            RDRQ;
1193  000531            B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % SAVE GPR (NEXT INSTR)
1194  000531            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1195  000532            AB,OCTNR ALUF,Q-A ALUD,B  % COMPARE EXPONENTS
1196  000532            T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1197  000532            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1198  000533            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
1199  000533            IDBS,STS COMM,CLFF T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1200  000533            NED1 CONDENABL;
1201  000534            A,R5 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1202  000534            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
1203  000534            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1204  000535            A,R5 ALUF,A-1 ALUD,NONE
1205  000535            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDLC T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1206  000535            EQUAL CONDENABL;
1207  000536  %********************************************************************
1208  000536  % MEMORY OPERAND HAS SMALLEST EXPONENT, SHIFT DOWN MANTISSA
1209  000536            A,R6 B,R5 ALUF,MASKAB ALUD,NONE
1210  000536            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
1211  000536            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1212  000537  %********************************************************************
1213  000537  % TEST IF MEMORY OPERAND INSIGNIFICANT, ANSWER = TAD
1214  000537            B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1215  000537            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1216  000537            STSST CONDENABL;
1217  000540            ALUD,NONE
1218  000540            IDBS,DSABL T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1219  000540            RDRQ;
1220  000541            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1221  000541            IDBS,DBR COMM,CLFF T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1222  000541            COND,LC=0;
1223  000542  %********************************************************************
1224  000542  % SHIFT DOWN MEMORY OPERAND MANTISSA
1225  000542            B,R3 MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD STS,ES
1226  000542            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,LOAD LCOUNT CYCL,FAST
1227  000542            DUMM2;
1228  000543            ALUD,NONE
1229  000543            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1230  000543            SUBF CONDENABL;
1231  000544  %********************************************************************
1232  000544  % EQUAL SIGNS ON OPERANDS, ADD MANTISSAS
1233  000544  ADDF:     A,D B,D ALUF,A+Q ALUD,B STS,EA
1234  000544            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1235  000545  ADDF2:    A,R3 B,A ALUF,A+B ALUD,B STS,EA CRY,C
1236  000545            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1237  000545            COND,CRY F,JMP F,HOLD;
1238  000546            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1239  000546            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1240  000546            TGOOD CONDENABL;
1241  000547  %********************************************************************
1242  000547  % ADDITION GAVE CARRY, SHIFT MANTISSA RIGHT
1243  000547            ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1244  000547            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1245  000547            1;
1246  000550            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
1247  000550            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1248  000551            B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1249  000551            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1250  000552            B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1251  000552            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1252  000552            40000;
1253  000553            A,T ALUF,A+1 ALUD,Q  % CHECK OVERFLOW
1254  000553            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1255  000553            CHKOF;
1256  000554            A,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1257  000554            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1258  000554            STSEX;
1259  000555  %********************************************************************
1260  000555  % TAD-OPERAND HAS SMALLEST EXPONENT, SHIFT DOWN MANTISSA
1261  000555  NED1:     A,R5 B,R4 ALUF,A-1 ALUD,B
1262  000555            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1263  000556            A,R6 B,R4 ALUF,ORAB ALUD,Q
1264  000556            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1265  000557            ALUF,INVQ ALUD,NONE
1266  000557            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
1267  000557            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1268  000560  %********************************************************************
1269  000560  % TEST IF TAD-OPERAND INSIGNIFICANT, ANSWER = MEMORY OPERAND
1270  000560            A,R5 ALUF,A+1 ALUD,NONE
1271  000560            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1272  000560            NORM2 CONDENABL;
1273  000561  %********************************************************************
1274  000561  % EXCHANGE OPERANDS
1275  000561            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1276  000561            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1277  000562            A,A B,R3 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1278  000562            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1279  000563            B,R4 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1280  000563            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1281  000563            RDRQ;
1282  000564            B,R5 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1283  000564            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1284  000565            A,R4 B,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1285  000565            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1286  000566            A,R5 B,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1287  000566            IDBS,ALU COMM,CLFF T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1288  000566            COND,LC=0;
1289  000567  %********************************************************************
1290  000567  % SHIFT DOWN TAD-OPERAND
1291  000567            B,R3 MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD STS,ES
1292  000567            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,LOAD LCOUNT CYCL,FAST
1293  000567            DUMM2;
1294  000570            A,R2 B,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1295  000570            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1296  000570            SUBF CONDENABL;
1297  000571            A,D B,D ALUF,A+Q ALUD,B STS,EA
1298  000571            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1299  000571            ADDF2;
1300  000572  %********************************************************************
1301  000572  % ANSWER = MEMORY OPERAND
1302  000572  NORM2:    B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1303  000572            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1304  000572            RDRQ;
1305  000573            B,D ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1306  000573            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1307  000574            A,R2 B,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1308  000574            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1309  000574            STSST;
1310  000575  %********************************************************************
1311  000575  % DIFFERENT SIGNS ON OPERANDS, SUBTRACT MANTISSAS
1312  000575  SUBF:     A,D B,D ALUF,A-Q ALUD,B STS,EA
1313  000575            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1314  000576            A,R3 B,A ALUF,B-A-1 ALUD,B CRY,C
1315  000576            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1316  000576            COND,OOD F,JMP F,HOLD;
1317  000577            A,STS ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1318  000577            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1319  000577            FAD3 CONDENABL;
1320  000600            B,STS ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,B  % SET TG-FLIP-FLOP
1321  000600            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1322  000600            2;
1323  000601            A,D B,D ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B
1324  000601            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1325  000602  FAD3:     A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1326  000602            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1327  000602            NOINV;
1328  000603  %********************************************************************
1329  000603  % EQUAL EXPONENTS
1330  000603  EQUAL:    A,R2 B,T ALUF,XORAB ALUD,NONE
1331  000603            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1332  000603            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1333  000604            ALUD,NONE
1334  000604            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1335  000604            SUBF3 CONDENABL;
1336  000605  %********************************************************************
1337  000605  % EQUAL SIGNS, ADD MANTISSAS
1338  000605            B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1339  000605            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1340  000605            RDRQ;
1341  000606            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1342  000606            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1343  000606            ADDF;
1344  000607  %********************************************************************
1345  000607  % DIFFERENT SIGNS, SUBTRACT MANTISSAS
1346  000607  SUBF3:    B,R3 ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1347  000607            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1348  000607            RDRQ;
1349  000610            A,D ALUF,A-D-1 ALUD,Q STS,EA
1350  000610            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1351  000611            A,R3 B,A ALUF,A+B ALUD,B STS,EA CRY,C
1352  000611            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1353  000611            COND,CRY F,JMP F,HOLD;
1354  000612            ALUD,NONE
1355  000612            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1356  000612            INV CONDENABL;
1357  000613            ALUF,Q+1 ALUD,Q STS,EA
1358  000613            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1359  000614            B,A ALUF,B ALUD,B CRY,C
1360  000614            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1361  000614            NOINV;
1362  000615  % INVERT ANSWER AFTER SUBTRACTION
1363  000615  INV:      ALUF,INVQ ALUD,Q
1364  000615            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1365  000615            100000;
1366  000616            A,T B,T ALUF,XORDA ALUD,B
1367  000616            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1368  000617            B,A ALUF,INVB ALUD,B
1369  000617            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1370  000620  %********************************************************************
1371  000620  % NORMALIZE ANSWER AFTER SUBTRACTION
1372  000620  NOINV:    B,R4 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1373  000620            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD
1374  000620            21;
1375  000621            A,2 B,A ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
1376  000621            IDBS,AARG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD
1377  000621            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1378  000622            A,T B,R5 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1379  000622            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1380  000622            FAD4 CONDENABL;
1381  000623            A,T B,R5 ALUF,A-D ALUD,B
1382  000623            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD;
1383  000624            B,A ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1384  000624            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
1385  000624            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1386  000625            ALUF,ZERO ALUD,Q
1387  000625            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1388  000625            FAD4 CONDENABL;
1389  000626  %********************************************************************
1390  000626  % ANSWER = 0
1391  000626  ZTAD:     B,A ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1392  000626            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1393  000627            B,D ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1394  000627            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1395  000630            A,R7 B,T ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B,YA
1396  000630            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1397  000630            STSEX;
1398  000631  % CONTINUED NORMALIZE AFTER SUBTRACTION
1399  000631  FAD4:     A,16 B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1400  000631            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1401  000631            COND,F15;
1402  000632            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLD
1403  000632            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT CYCL,FAST;
1404  000633            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
1405  000633            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1406  000634            B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
1407  000634            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1408  000635            B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1409  000635            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
1410  000636            A,R4 B,LC ALUF,A-D-1 ALUD,Q
1411  000636            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD;
1412  000637            A,R5 ALUF,A-Q ALUD,Q  % CHECK IF UNDERFLOW
1413  000637            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1414  000637            CHKUF;
1415  000640            A,R7 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE  % RESTORE GPR
1416  000640            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1417  000640            STSEX;
1418  000641  % SUBROUTINE TO CORRECT FOR OVERSHOOT AFTER MANTISSA ALIGNMENT
1419  000641  DUMM2:    B,R3 MIS,LIN ALUF,PASSB ALUD,SLD
1420  000641            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1421  000642            A,R2 B,T ALUF,XORAB ALUD,NONE
1422  000642            IDBS,ALU T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,FAST
1423  000642            COND,F15 F,NEXT;
1424  000643  %********************************************************************
1425  000643  %********************************************************************
1426  000643  % FLOATING MULTIPLY ROUTINE
1427  000643  %********************************************************************
1428  000661  661/
1429  000661  FMU:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1430  000661            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1431  000661            MREFL;
1432  000662            B,STS ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1433  000662            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1434  000662            2;
1435  000663            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
1436  000663            IDBS,STS T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1437  000664            B,R2 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1438  000664            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1439  000664            RDRQ;
1440  000665            A,R2 B,T ALUF,A+B ALUD,Q  % ADD EXPONENTS
1441  000665            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1442  000666            A,17 ALUF,MASKDQ ALUD,Q
1443  000666            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1444  000667            B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1445  000667            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1446  000667            RDRQ;
1447  000670            A,T B,R2 ALUF,XORAB ALUD,B
1448  000670            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1449  000670            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1450  000671            B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1451  000671            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1452  000671            FMU2 CONDENABL;
1453  000672  %********************************************************************
1454  000672  % UNEQUAL SIGNS ON OPERANDS
1455  000672            A,17 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1456  000672            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1457  000673  FMU2:     ALUD,NONE
1458  000673            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1459  000673            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1460  000674            B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1461  000674            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1462  000674            40000;
1463  000675            A,R2 B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1464  000675            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
1465  000676            A,T B,R2 ALUF,XNORAQ ALUD,B
1466  000676            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1467  000676            FMU1 CONDENABL;
1468  000677            A,R2 B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1469  000677            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
1470  000700  %********************************************************************
1471  000700  % OFUF MEANS EITHER OVER- OR UNDER-FLOW AFTER EXPONENT ADD
1472  000700            ALUD,NONE
1473  000700            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1474  000700            OFUF CONDENABL;
1475  000701  %********************************************************************
1476  000701  % MULTIPLY MANTISSAS
1477  000701  FMU1:     A,16 B,T ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,B
1478  000701            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1479  000702            ALUF,ZERO ALUD,Q
1480  000702            IDBS,ALU COMM,CLFF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1481  000702            COND,LC=0;
1482  000703            B,R1 ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
1483  000703            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1484  000703            17;
1485  000704            A,D ALUF,A+Q ALUM,FMU ALUD,Q STS,EA
1486  000704            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1487  000705            A,A B,R1 MIS,ZIN ALUF,A+B ALUM,FMU ALUD,SRD STS,EA CRY,C
1488  000705            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT;
1489  000706            A,R3 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1490  000706            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1491  000707            ALUD,NONE
1492  000707            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1493  000707            16;
1494  000710            A,D ALUF,A+Q ALUM,FMU ALUD,Q STS,EA
1495  000710            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1496  000711            A,A B,R1 MIS,ZIN ALUF,A+B ALUM,FMU ALUD,SRD STS,EA CRY,C
1497  000711            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT;
1498  000712            A,D ALUF,A+Q ALUM,FMU ALUD,Q STS,EA
1499  000712            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1500  000713            A,A B,R1 ALUF,A+B ALUM,FMU ALUD,B STS,EA CRY,C
1501  000713            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1502  000713            COND,CRY F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1503  000714            A,R1 B,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
1504  000714            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1505  000714            MCRY1 CONDENABL;
1506  000715  %********************************************************************
1507  000715  % NO CARRY IN LAST ADDITION
1508  000715            A,R1 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1509  000715            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
1510  000715            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1511  000716            B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1512  000716            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1513  000716            ZTAD CONDENABL;
1514  000717            A,T ALUF,A-1 ALUD,Q  % CHECK UNDERFLOW
1515  000717            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1516  000717            CHKUF;
1517  000720  %********************************************************************
1518  000720  % CHECK TG-BEHAVIOUR
1519  000720  TGOOD:    ALUD,NONE
1520  000720            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1521  000720            COND,OOD F,JMP F,HOLD;
1522  000721  TGTST:    A,STS ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1523  000721            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1524  000721            STSST CONDENABL;
1525  000722  TGSET:    B,STS ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,B  % SET TG
1526  000722            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1527  000722            2;
1528  000723            A,D B,D ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B
1529  000723            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1530  000723            STSST;
1531  000724  %********************************************************************
1532  000724  % THERE WAS CARRY FROM LAST ADDITION IN MULTIPLY LOOP
1533  000724  MCRY1:    B,1 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1534  000724            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1535  000724            COND,OOD F,JMP F,HOLD;
1536  000725  %********************************************************************
1537  000725  % SHIFT DOWN MANTISSAS
1538  000725            A,1 B,A MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SRD
1539  000725            IDBS,BMG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1540  000725            FMU3 CONDENABL;
1541  000726            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B
1542  000726            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1543  000727            ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1544  000727            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1545  000727            1;
1546  000730  FMU3:     A,R7 B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B,YA  % RESTORE GPR
1547  000730            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1548  000730            STSEX;
1549  000731  %********************************************************************
1550  000731  %********************************************************************
1551  000731  % FLOATING DIVIDE ROUTINE
1552  000731  %********************************************************************
1553  000740  740/
1554  000740  FDV:      B,R1 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
1555  000740            IDBS,GPR,SEXT T,JMP8-10 T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
1556  000740            MREFL;
1557  000741            B,STS ALUF,INVD ALUD,B
1558  000741            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1559  000741            2;
1560  000742            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
1561  000742            IDBS,STS T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1562  000743            B,R2 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1563  000743            IDBS,DBR T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
1564  000743            RDRQ;
1565  000744  %********************************************************************
1566  000744  % SUBTRACT EXPONENTS
1567  000744            A,T B,R2 ALUF,A-B ALUD,Q
1568  000744            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1569  000745            A,17 ALUF,MASKDQ ALUD,Q
1570  000745            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1571  000746            A,T B,R2 ALUF,XORAB ALUD,B
1572  000746            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1573  000746            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1574  000747            B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1575  000747            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1576  000747            FDV2 CONDENABL;
1577  000750  %********************************************************************
1578  000750  % UNEQUAL SIGNS ON OPERANDS
1579  000750            A,17 ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
1580  000750            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1581  000751  FDV2:     B,R3 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1582  000751            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1583  000751            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1584  000752            A,16 ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,Q
1585  000752            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1586  000752            FLOVF CONDENABL;
1587  000753            B,R1 ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
1588  000753            IDBS,ALU MIS,HOLD COMM,RDRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1589  000754            B,R4 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1590  000754            IDBS,DBR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
1591  000754            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1592  000755            B,R6 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
1593  000755            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1594  000755            40000;
1595  000756            A,R2 B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1596  000756            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
1597  000757            A,T B,R2 ALUF,XORAQ ALUD,B
1598  000757            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1599  000757            FDV3 CONDENABL;
1600  000760            A,R2 B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1601  000760            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
1602  000761            ALUD,NONE
1603  000761            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1604  000761            FDV3 CONDENABL;
1605  000762  %********************************************************************
1606  000762  % OVERFLOW OR UNDERFLOW IN FLOATING OPERATION
1607  000762  OFUF:     A,T B,R6 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
1608  000762            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1609  000762            COND,F=0 F,HOLD;
1610  000763            ALUD,NONE
1611  000763            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1612  000763            FLOVF CONDENABL;
1613  000764            ALUD,NONE
1614  000764            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1615  000764            ZTAD;
1616  000765  %********************************************************************
1617  000765  % DIVIDE MANTISSAS
1618  000765  FDV3:     B,T ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B
1619  000765            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1620  000765            16;
1621  000766            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
1622  000766            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1623  000766            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1624  000767            A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1625  000767            IDBS,ALU COMM,CLFF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1626  000767            ZTAD CONDENABL;
1627  000770            A,7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE STS,LO
1628  000770            IDBS,BMG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1629  000771            A,R4 ALUF,Q-A ALUD,Q STS,EA
1630  000771            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1631  000772            A,R3 B,A MIS,ZIN ALUF,B-A-1 ALUD,SLD STS,ES CRY,C
1632  000772            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,PUSH
1633  000772            COND,LC=0;

1635  000773            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;

1637  000774            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,POP LCOUNT;
1638  000775            B,R5 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B

1640  000776            A,R5 B,17 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE

1643  000777            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1644  001000            A,R3 B,A MIS,ZIN ALUF,B-A-1 ALUM,FDV ALUD,SLD STS,ES CRY,C

1648  001001            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
1649  001002  %********************************************************************

1656  001003            CHKOF;
1657  001004            ALUD,NONE

1667  001007            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1668  001007            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1669  001007            2;
1670  001010            A,STS B,STS ALUF,ORAQ ALUD,B

1676  001012            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1677  001012            STSEX;
1678  001013  %********************************************************************
1679  001013  % NORMALIZE ANSWER IN FLOATING DIVIDE
1680  001013  POSDV:    A,D ALUF,PASSA ALUD,Q
1681  001013            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1682  001014            B,A ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLD
1683  001014            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1684  001014            COND,DZD F,JMP F,HOLD;

1692  001016            77777;

1695  001020            A,Z B,D ALUF,INVA ALUD,B

1718  001030            A,T B,R2 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,B

1720  001030            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;

1854  001115  %********************************************************************
1855  001115  %********************************************************************
1856  001115  % SUBROUTINE USED BY IOX OR MICROPROGRAMMED LOADERS
1857  001115  % TO ISSUE IOXINSTRUCTIONS ON SYSTEM BUS
1858  001115  %********************************************************************
1859  001115  % IOX-ROUTINE FOR 11-BIT IOX-ADDRESS
1860  001115  IOXG:     ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1861  001115            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1862  001115            3777;
1863  001116            A,R1 B,R1 ALUF,ANDAQ ALUD,B
1864  001116            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1865  001117  %********************************************************************

1867  001117  IOXX1:    A,R1 B,7 ALUF,MASKDA ALUD,Q
1868  001117            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1869  001117            COND,OPCTT1 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1870  001120  %********************************************************************
1871  001120  % CHECK IF DEVICE NUMBERS 30X IS ON THE CPU-BOARD
1872  001120            ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,NONE
1873  001120            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
1874  001120            300;
1875  001121            A,R1 B,3 ALUF,MASKDA ALUD,Q
1876  001121            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1877  001121            TERMX CONDENABL;
1878  001122  %********************************************************************
1879  001122  % CHECK IF DEVICE IS IN THE RANGE 10-13
1880  001122            B,10 ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,NONE
1881  001122            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
1882  001122            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1883  001123            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1884  001123            IDBS,IOR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1885  001123            CLKX CONDENABL;
1886  001124  %********************************************************************
1887  001124  % DEVICE IS NOT ON CPU-BOARD. ISSUE GENERAL IOX
1888  001124            A,R1 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE  % ADDRESS -> IOX
1889  001124            COMM,IOX T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1890  001125            A,A ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE  % OUTPUT DATA
1891  001125            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
1892  001126            B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % INPUT DATA
1893  001126            IDBS,DBR T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,WAIT;
1894  001127  %********************************************************************
1895  001127  % DEV.NO. 30X IS ON CPU BOARD. JUMP TO VECTOR
1896  001127  TERMX:    AB,STATUS ALUF,PASSD
1897  001127            IDBS,REG T,JMP0-3 T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1898  001127            TRMVC;
1899  001130  %********************************************************************
1900  001130  % DEV.NO. IS 10-13, JUMP TO VECTOR
1901  001130  CLKX:     AB,STATUS ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
1902  001130            IDBS,REG T,JMP0-3 T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
1903  001130            CLKVC;
1904  001131  %********************************************************************
1905  001131  % SUBROUTINE TO WRITE STATUS-SCRATCH WORD, AND TO WRITE SIOC-REGISTER
1906  001131  WSIOC:    AB,STATUS ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
1907  001131            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
1908  001132  WSIO2:    ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
1909  001132            IDBS,ALU COMM,SIOC T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW;

1915  001133            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD
1916  001133            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
1917  001134            B,A ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,B
1918  001134            IDBS,ARG T,RETURN T,POP
1919  001134            377 CONDENABL;
1920  001135            ALUD,NONE
1921  001135            IDBS,ALU COMM,RSDA T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,SLOW;

1923  001136  % IOX 302

3139  001711            A,R5 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE

3141  001711            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3142  001712            AB,MACL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3143  001712            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD
3144  001712            STOP2 CONDENABL;
3145  001713  %********************************************************************
3146  001713  % PANEL IS LOCKED WHEN POWER-UP, OR LOAD IS PRESSED
3147  001713  ALDLO:    ALUD,NONE  % USE ALD-SWITCH
3148  001713            IDBS,ALU T,JMPALD T,PUSH
3149  001713            ALDVC;
3150  001714  %********************************************************************
3151  001714  % LOAD, $ OR &, LOAD CODE IN Q
3152  001714  LOAD1:    A,17 ALUF,MASKDQ ALUD,Q
3153  001714            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3154  001714            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3155  001715            A,15 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
3156  001715            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD
3157  001715            INIT CONDENABL;
3158  001716  %********************************************************************
3159  001716  % Q IS NOT 0
3160  001716            A,7 B,STS ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
3161  001716            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD
3162  001716            ETLO1 CONDENABL;
3163  001717  %********************************************************************
3164  001717  %********************************************************************
3165  001717  % MASS STORAGE LOAD, BECAUSE BIT 13 IS 1
3166  001717  %********************************************************************
3167  001717  MASS:     A,1 B,D ALUF,D+Q ALUD,B  % DEV.NO. +2 -> D
3168  001717            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD;
3169  001720  MAS1:     B,A ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
3170  001720            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
3171  001721  %********************************************************************
3172  001721  % IOX N+1 (0) CORE ADDRESS
3173  001721            A,D B,R1 ALUF,A-1 ALUD,B
3174  001721            T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
3175  001721            IOXG;
3176  001722  %********************************************************************
3177  001722  % IOX N+1 (0) CORE ADDRESS
3178  001722            A,D B,R1 ALUF,A-1 ALUD,B
3179  001722            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3180  001722            IOXG;
3181  001723  %********************************************************************
3182  001723  % IOX N+3 (0) BLOCK ADDRESS
3183  001723            A,D B,R1 ALUF,A+1 ALUD,B
3184  001723            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3185  001723            IOXG;
3186  001724  %********************************************************************
3187  001724  % IOX N+7 (2000) WORD COUNTER
3188  001724            A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A ALUD,B
3189  001724            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3190  001724            5;
3191  001725            A,12 B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3192  001725            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH
3193  001725            IOXG;
3194  001726  %********************************************************************
3195  001726  % IOX N+5 (4) ACTIVATE DEVICE
3196  001726            A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A+1 ALUD,B
3197  001726            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD;
3198  001727            A,2 B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3199  001727            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH
3200  001727            IOXG;
3201  001730  %********************************************************************
3202  001730  % IOX N+4 READ STATUS
3203  001730  MAS2:     A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A ALUD,B
3204  001730            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH
3205  001730            IOXG;
3206  001731            A,A B,4 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,NONE
3207  001731            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3208  001731            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3209  001732            A,2 B,R7 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3210  001732            IDBS,AARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3211  001732            MAS2 CONDENABL;
3212  001733  %********************************************************************
3213  001733  % DEVICE FINISHED
3214  001733            A,A B,R7 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
3215  001733            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
3216  001734            ALUD,NONE
3217  001734            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3218  001734            MAS1 CONDENABL;
3219  001735  %********************************************************************
3220  001735  % NO ERRORS, START IN 0, AND LEAVE MOPC
3221  001735            B,P ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
3222  001735            IDBS,ALU COMM,START T,JMP T,HOLD
3223  001735            ESCAP;
3224  001736  %********************************************************************
3225  001736  %********************************************************************
3226  001736  % RESTART, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3766)
3227  001736  % ONLY IF STAND-BY POWER IS OK, OR RESTART BUTTON PRESSED
3228  001736  %********************************************************************
3229  001736  RSTRT:    ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3230  001736            IDBS,IOR COMM,CLRTC T,NEXT T,HOLD
3231  001736            COND,STP F,RETURN F,POP;
3232  001737            A,15 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,Q
3233  001737            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3234  001737            CONDENABL COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3235  001740  %********************************************************************
3236  001740  % ONLY IF IN STOP MODE
3237  001740            AB,MACL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
3238  001740            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3239  001740            RSTR1 CONDENABL;
3240  001741  %********************************************************************
3241  001741  % IF LOCKED
3242  001741            AB,MACL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3243  001741            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMPALD T,PUSH
3244  001741            ALDVC;
3245  001742            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3246  001742            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3247  001742            COND,F15 F,NEXT;
3248  001743  %********************************************************************
3249  001743  % LOAD IF BIT 15=1, START IN ADDRESS 20 IF BIT 15=0
3250  001743            ALUD,NONE
3251  001743            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3252  001743            LOAD1 CONDENABL;
3253  001744  %********************************************************************
3254  001744  % START CPU IN ADDRESS 20
3255  001744  RSTR2:    A,2 B,P ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3256  001744            IDBS,AARG T,JMP T,HOLD
3257  001744            CONT;
3258  001745  %********************************************************************
3259  001745  % IF UNLOCKED, DO NOTHING JUST AFTER MASTER CLEAR, ELSE START IN 20
3260  001745  RSTR1:    AB,MACL ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3261  001745            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD
3262  001745            INIT CONDENABL;
3263  001746            ALUD,NONE
3264  001746            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3265  001746            RSTR2;
3266  001747  %********************************************************************
3267  001747  %********************************************************************
3268  001747  % LOAD BUTTON IS PRESSED, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3764 OR 3774)
3269  001747  %********************************************************************
3270  001747  LOAD:     ALUD,NONE
3271  001747            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3272  001747            COND,STP F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3273  001750            ALUD,NONE
3274  001750            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3275  001750            ALDLO CONDENABL;
3276  001751  %********************************************************************
3277  001751  %********************************************************************
3278  001751  % CPU IS NOT IN STOP WHEN LOAD IS PRESSED, OR
3279  001751  % CONTINUE BUTTON IS PRESSED, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3765 OR 3775)
3280  001751  %********************************************************************
3281  001751  CONT:     ALUD,NONE
3282  001751            IDBS,ALU COMM,START T,JMP T,HOLD
3283  001751            ESCAP;
3284  001752  %********************************************************************
3285  001752  %********************************************************************
3286  001752  % $ OR & IS TYPED TO MOPC
3287  001752  %********************************************************************
3288  001752  DOLOA:
3289  001752  ETLOA:    AB,OCTNR ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3290  001752            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3291  001752            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3292  001753  %********************************************************************
3293  001753  % USE ALD-SWITCH IF NO OCTAL NUMBER GIVEN
3294  001753            ALUD,NONE
3295  001753            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3296  001753            ALDLO CONDENABL;
3297  001754            ALUD,NONE
3298  001754            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3299  001754            LOAD1;
3300  001755  %********************************************************************
3301  001755  %********************************************************************
3302  001755  % BINARY LOADER. LOAD CODE BIT 13 IS 0.
3303  001755  %********************************************************************
3304  001755  ETLO1:    B,D ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,B  % DEV.NO. -> D
3305  001755            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3306  001755            DVACT;
3307  001756  SEEK:     A,R5 B,P ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B  % POSSIBLE S.A.
3308  001756            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
3309  001757  SIKI:     A,5 B,L ALUF,D+1 ALUD,B  % READ OCTAL NO.
3310  001757            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH
3311  001757            ASS8;
3312  001760            A,R2 B,15 ALUF,A-D ALUD,NONE
3313  001760            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3314  001760            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3315  001761            A,R2 B,L ALUF,A-B ALUD,NONE
3316  001761            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3317  001761            SEEK CONDENABL;
3318  001762  %********************************************************************
3319  001762  % LAST CHAR. IS NOT CR
3320  001762            ALUD,NONE
3321  001762            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3322  001762            EXFOU CONDENABL;
3323  001763  %********************************************************************
3324  001763  % LAST CHAR. IS NOT !
3325  001763            ALUD,NONE
3326  001763            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3327  001763            SIKI;
3328  001764  %********************************************************************
3329  001764  % EXCLAMATION MARK (!) FOUND
3330  001764  EXFOU:    A,STS B,STS ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
3331  001764            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
3332  001765            B,L ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B  % READ CORE ADDR.
3333  001765            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3334  001765            BIN;
3335  001766            A,Z B,X ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B  % READ WORD COUNT
3336  001766            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3337  001766            BIN;
3338  001767            A,Z B,T ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B
3339  001767            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
3340  001770  %********************************************************************
3341  001770  % LOOP TO STORE PROGRAM IN MEMORY
3342  001770  STLP:     ALUD,NONE  % READ ONE WORD
3343  001770            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
3344  001770            BIN;
3345  001771            B,T ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B  % DECR. WORD COUNT
3346  001771            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3347  001771            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3348  001772            A,X B,X ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B,YA  % INCR. CORE ADDR.
3349  001772            IDBS,ALU MIS,PT COMM,WRRQ T,NEXT T,HOLD
3350  001772            COND,COND F,JMP F,HOLD;
3351  001773            A,Z B,L ALUF,A+B ALUD,B,YA  % ACCUMULATE CH.SM.
3352  001773            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
3353  001773            STLP CONDENABL;
3354  001774  %********************************************************************
3355  001774  % ALL WORDS ARE PLACED IN MEMORY
3356  001774            ALUD,NONE  % READ CHECKSUM
3357  001774            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,WAIT
3358  001774            BIN;
3359  001775            A,Z B,L ALUF,XORAB ALUD,NONE  % TEST CH.SM.
3360  001775            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3361  001775            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3362  001776            ALUD,NONE
3363  001776            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3364  001776            ILLEG CONDENABL;
3365  001777  %********************************************************************
3366  001777  % CHECKSUM OK, READ ACTION CODE
3367  001777            ALUD,NONE
3368  001777            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3369  001777            ASS8;
3370  002000            A,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
3371  002000            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3372  002000            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3373  002001            ALUD,NONE
3374  002001            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3375  002001            ESCAP CONDENABL;
3376  002002  %********************************************************************
3377  002002  % ACTION CODE =0, START PROGRAM
3378  002002            ALUD,NONE
3379  002002            IDBS,ALU COMM,START T,JMP T,HOLD
3380  002002            ESCAP;
3381  002003  %********************************************************************
3382  002003  % SUBROUTINE FOR BINARY LOADER TO READ ONE CHARACTER
3383  002003  INCH:     A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A ALUD,B  % IOX N+2
3384  002003            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,PUSH
3385  002003            IOXG;
3386  002004            A,A B,10 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,NONE
3387  002004            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3388  002004            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3389  002005            ALUD,NONE
3390  002005            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3391  002005            INCH CONDENABL;
3392  002006  %********************************************************************
3393  002006  % READY FOR TRANSFER
3394  002006            A,D B,R1 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,B  % IOX N
3395  002006            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3396  002006            IOXG;
3397  002007            A,A B,STS ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,NONE
3398  002007            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD
3399  002007            DVACT;
3400  002010  %********************************************************************
3401  002010  % ACTIVATE DEVICE
3402  002010  DVACT:    B,A ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3403  002010            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3404  002010            4005;
3405  002011            A,D B,R1 ALUF,D+A+1 ALUD,B  % IOX N+3 (4005)
3406  002011            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD
3407  002011            IOXG;
3408  002012  %********************************************************************
3409  002012  % SUBROUTINE FOR BINARY LOADER TO READ OCTAL NUMBER, RETURNS ON NON-OCTAL CHAR
3410  002012  ASS8:     AB,OCTNR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,B
3411  002012            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD
3412  002012            ASS81;
3413  002013  %********************************************************************
3414  002013  % OCTAL DIGIT FOUND
3415  002013  ASS82:    ALUD,NONE
3416  002013            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3417  002013            OCDIG;
3418  002014  ASS81:    AB,OCTNR ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B  % READ CHARACTER
3419  002014            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH
3420  002014            INCH;
3421  002015            A,STS B,R2 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
3422  002015            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD
3423  002015            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3424  002016            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3425  002016            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3426  002016            60;
3427  002017            A,R2 B,R3 ALUF,A-Q ALUD,B
3428  002017            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD;
3429  002020            A,R3 B,10 ALUF,A-D ALUD,NONE
3430  002020            IDBS,BARG T,RETURN T,POP
3431  002020            CONDENABL COND,F15 F,RETURN F,POP;
3432  002021            ALUD,NONE
3433  002021            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3434  002021            ASS82 CONDENABL;
3435  002022  %********************************************************************
3436  002022  % SUBROUTINE USED BY BINARY LOADER TO READ TWO BYTES AS ONE 16-BIT WORD
3437  002022  BIN:      ALUD,NONE  % READ BYTE 1
3438  002022            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3439  002022            INCH;
3440  002023            ALUD,NONE  % SWAP BYTES
3441  002023            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD;
3442  002024            B,Z ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
3443  002024            IDBS,SWAP T,JMP T,PUSH
3444  002024            INCH;
3445  002025            A,Z B,Z ALUF,ORDA ALUD,B  % MAKE WORD
3446  002025            IDBS,GPR T,RETURN T,POP;
3447  002026  %********************************************************************
3448  002026  %********************************************************************
3449  002026  % ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR EVERY 20 MS
3450  002026  %********************************************************************
3451  002026  MS20:     B,10 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3452  002026            IDBS,BARG COMM,CLRTC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
3453  002027            AB,STATUS ALUF,ORDQ ALUD,Q
3454  002027            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH
3455  002027            WSIOC;
3456  002030  %********************************************************************
3457  002030  % TEST IF 20 MS CLOCK SHOULD ACTIVATE MOPC
3458  002030            ALUD,NONE
3459  002030            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDIR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3460  002030            150040;
3461  002031            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
3462  002031            IDBS,MMR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3463  002031            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
3464  002032            ALUD,NONE
3465  002032            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3466  002032            MOPC CONDENABL;
3467  002033  %********************************************************************
3468  002033  % MOPC SHOULD BE DRIVEN BY PANEL REQUEST FROM MEMORY MANAGEMENT MODULE
3469  002033            ALUD,NONE
3470  002033            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD
3471  002033            INIT;
3472  002034  %********************************************************************
3473  002034  %********************************************************************
3474  002034  % PANEL REQUEST, ENTERED FROM INTERRUPT VECTOR (3762)
3475  002034  %********************************************************************
3476  002034  PRQ:      ALUD,NONE
3477  002034            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDIR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3478  002034            150040;
3479  002035            ALUD,NONE
3480  002035            IDBS,MMR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW;
3481  002036  %********************************************************************
3482  002036  %********************************************************************
3483  002036  % MAIN ENTRY POINT OF MOPC
3484  002036  %********************************************************************
3485  002036  MOPC:
3486  002036  MRET1:    AB,PRCHR ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
3487  002036            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3488  002036            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3489  002037            ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3490  002037            IDBS,IOR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3491  002037            NOUTP CONDENABL;
3492  002040  %********************************************************************
3493  002040  % CHARACTER IS WAITING TO BE OUTPUTTED
3494  002040            A,16 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
3495  002040            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD
3496  002040            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3497  002041            A,17 ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,NONE
3498  002041            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD
3499  002041            OUTPT CONDENABL;
3500  002042  %********************************************************************
3501  002042  % OUTPUT STOPPED BY INPUT
3502  002042            ALUD,NONE
3503  002042            IDBS,ALU COMM,RSDA T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3504  002042            RONLY;
3505  002043  OUTPT:    ALUD,NONE
3506  002043            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3507  002043            INIT CONDENABL;
3508  002044  %********************************************************************
3509  002044  % UART READY FOR OUTPUT
3510  002044            AB,PRCHR ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3511  002044            IDBS,REG COMM,TBSTR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3512  002044            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3513  002045            ALUF,D-Q ALUD,NONE
3514  002045            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD
3515  002045            43;
3516  002046  %********************************************************************
3517  002046  % JUMP TO CR IF OUTPUTTED CHAR. WAS #
3518  002046            B,12 ALUF,D-Q ALUD,NONE
3519  002046            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD
3520  002046            CR CONDENABL;
3521  002047  %********************************************************************
3522  002047  % JUMP TO LFEED IF OUTPUTTED CHAR. WAS LF
3523  002047            AB,PRCHR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3524  002047            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD
3525  002047            LFEED CONDENABL;
3526  002050            AB,CDIGI ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3527  002050            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3528  002050            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3529  002051            ALUD,NONE
3530  002051            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3531  002051            OUTP1 CONDENABL;
3532  002052  %********************************************************************
3533  002052  % THERE ARE MORE CHARACTERS LEFT TO BE PRINTED IN ONE OCTAL NUMBER
3534  002052            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3535  002052            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
3536  002052            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3537  002053            AB,CDIGI ALUF,Q-1 ALUD,NONE
3538  002053            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3539  002053            SPAC3 CONDENABL;
3540  002054  %********************************************************************
3541  002054  % GET NEXT DIGIT
3542  002054            AB,NUMBR MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSD ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLB
3543  002054            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD;
3544  002055            AB,NUMBR MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLB
3545  002055            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
3546  002056            AB,NUMBR MIS,ROT ALUF,PASSB ALUM,MIC ALUD,SLB
3547  002056            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
3548  002057            AB,NUMBR ALUF,PASSB ALUD,Q
3549  002057            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
3550  002060            ALUF,ANDDQ ALUD,Q
3551  002060            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3552  002060            7;
3553  002061            ALUF,D+Q ALUD,Q
3554  002061            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3555  002061            60;
3556  002062  %********************************************************************
3557  002062  % PLACE IT IN PRCHR
3558  002062  PRICH:    AB,PRCHR ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3559  002062            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3560  002062            2036;
3561  002063  %********************************************************************
3562  002063  % GET SPACE, WHICH IS THE LAST CHAR. IN AN OCTAL NUMBER
3563  002063  SPAC3:    A,5 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3564  002063            IDBS,BMG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3565  002063            PRICH;
3566  002064  %********************************************************************
3567  002064  % ONE OCTAL NUMBER IS FINISHED
3568  002064  OUTP1:    AB,DUMPF ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3569  002064            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3570  002064            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3571  002065            AB,CNT10 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3572  002065            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3573  002065            2036 CONDENABL;

3575  002066  % WE ARE PRESENTLY INSIDE A DUMP COMMAND
3576  002066            B,2 ALUF,Q-D ALUD,NONE
3577  002066            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3578  002066            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3579  002067            B,12 ALUF,Q-D ALUD,NONE
3580  002067            IDBS,BARG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3581  002067            DUMP2 CONDENABL;

3584  002070            DMP10 CONDENABL;
3585  002071  %********************************************************************
3586  002071  % SOMETHING ELSE THAN MEMORY CONTENT SHOULD BE PRINTED IN THE DUMP NOW
3587  002071  DUMP2:    AB,CNT10 ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
3588  002071            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3589  002071            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,PUSH;
3590  002072            ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE
3591  002072            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,PUSH
3592  002072            DUMP3;
3593  002073  %********************************************************************
3594  002073  % CNT10 = 0 , PRINT ADDRESS

3596  002073            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,PUSH CYCL,FAST
3597  002073            PROCT;
3598  002074            AB,CNT10 ALUF,D+1 ALUD,Q
3599  002074            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3600  002074            MRET3;

3602  002075  % NEXT OCTAL NUMBER SHOULD POSSIBLY BE PRINTED
3603  002075  DMP10:    AB,UPPNR ALUF,Q-D ALUD,NONE
3604  002075            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
3605  002075            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
3606  002076            AB,PRCHR ALUF,ZERO ALUD,NONE
3607  002076            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
3608  002076            SPACE CONDENABL;  % JUMP TO SPACE IF FINISHED WITH DUMP

5997  003545  %********************************************************************
5998  003545  % MOPC STOP-ROUTINE CONTINUED
5999  003545  STOP2:    AB,SINGL ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
6000  003545            IDBS,REG COMM,SSTOP T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
6001  003545            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
6002  003546            AB,BPFLG ALUF,PASSD ALUD,NONE
6003  003546            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
6004  003546            BPCHK CONDENABL;
6005  003547  %********************************************************************
6006  003547  % MULTIPLE SINGLE IS ON
6007  003547            AB,SINGL ALUF,Q-1 ALUD,NONE
6008  003547            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,SLOW
6009  003547            SINGL;
6010  003550  BPCHK:    AB,BRKPT ALUF,PASSD ALUD,Q
6011  003550            IDBS,REG T,JMP T,HOLD
6012  003550            MANIR CONDENABL;
6013  003551  %********************************************************************
6014  003551  % BREAKPOINT IS ON
6015  003551            A,P ALUF,Q-A ALUD,NONE
6016  003551            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD
6017  003551            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
6018  003552            A,6 ALUF,D-1 ALUD,Q
6019  003552            IDBS,AARG T,NEXT T,HOLD
6020  003552            SINGL CONDENABL;
6021  003553  %********************************************************************
6022  003553  % BREAKPOINT IS FINISHED, PRINT .
6023  003553            AB,PRCHR ALUF,Q-1 ALUD,NONE
6024  003553            IDBS,ALU COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD
6025  003553            RONLY;
6026  003554  %********************************************************************
6027  003554  % . PRINTED TO MOPC, SET UP BREAKPOINT MODE

6029  003554            IDBS,REG T,NEXT T,HOLD;
6030  003555            AB,BRKPT ALUF,PASSQ ALUD,NONE

6032  003556            AB,BPFLG ALUD,NONE
6033  003556            IDBS,ARG COMM,EWRF T,JMP T,HOLD
6034  003556            SPAC9;

6421  004002            COMD;

6423  004003            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
6424  004003            TSET;
6425  004004            ALUD,NONE  % CONVERT TO BCD
6426  004004            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
6427  004004            PACK;
6428  004005            ALUD,NONE  % CONVERT TO ASCII
6429  004005            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT

6435  004007            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
6436  004007            RDUS;
6437  004010            ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT BFILL;
6438  004011            ALUD,NONE IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT MOVB;

6453  004020  %********************************************************************
6454  004020  % DECIMAL SUBTRACTION SUBROUTINE
6455  004020  SSUB:     B,R5 ALUF,XORDQ ALUD,B
6456  004020            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6457  004021            ALUF,Q-D-1 ALUD,Q STS,EA CRY,C
6458  004021            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6459  004021            COND,CRY F,NEXT F,HOLD;
6460  004022            AB,OCTNR ALUF,XORAQ ALUD,B
6461  004022            T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6462  004022            SUBB CONDENABL;
6463  004023            ALUF,Q-D ALUD,Q
6464  004023            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6465  004023            60000;
6466  004024  SUBB:     A,L B,R5 ALUF,ANDAB ALUD,SRB
6467  004024            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6468  004025            A,R5 B,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,SRB

6470  004026            A,R2 B,R5 ALUF,ORAB ALUD,SRB
6471  004026            IDBS,ALU T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,FAST;
6472  004027  %********************************************************************

6475  004027            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6476  004030            ALUF,D+Q ALUD,Q
6477  004030            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6478  004030            63146;
6479  004031            ALUF,D+Q ALUD,Q STS,EA CRY,C
6480  004031            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6481  004031            COND,CRY F,NEXT F,HOLD;

6483  004032            T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6484  004032            ADDB CONDENABL;

6486  004033            IDBS,ARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6487  004033            60000;

6511  004044  %********************************************************************
6512  004044  % SUBROUTINE TO SAVE REGISTERS FROM CURRENT LEVEL
6513  004044  % L -> RF(5,13)
6514  004044  % B -> RF(5,12)
6515  004044  % GPR IS USED AS SCRATCH
6516  004044  CSAV1:    A,L ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
6517  004044            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6518  004045            A,5 B,13 ALUD,NONE
6519  004045            IDBS,GPR COMM,EWRF T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6520  004046            A,B ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
6521  004046            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6522  004047            A,A B,12 ALUD,NONE
6523  004047            IDBS,GPR COMM,EWRF T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,FAST;
6524  004050  %********************************************************************
6525  004050  % SUBROUTINE TO UNSAVE REGISTERS TO CURRENT LEVEL
6526  004050  % RF(5,13) -> L
6527  004050  % RF(5,12) -> B
6528  004050  % GPR IS USED AS SCRATCH
6529  004050  CUSV1:    A,5 B,12 ALUD,NONE
6530  004050            IDBS,REG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT;
6531  004051            B,B ALUF,PASSD ALUD,B
6532  004051            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6533  004052            A,5 B,13 ALUD,NONE
6534  004052            IDBS,REG COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,NORM;
