6536  004053            IDBS,GPR T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,FAST;
6537  004054  %********************************************************************
6538  004054  % SUBROUTINE TO COMPUTE BOTTOM WORD ADDRESS
6539  004054  % AND NUMBER OF WORDS OF AN ASCII OPERAND
6540  004054  % DESCRIPTORS IN R1,R2
6541  004054  % RETURN WITH NUMBER OF WORDS IN R4
6542  004054  % NUMBER OF BYTES IN R7
6543  004054  % BOTTOM WORD ADDRESS IN R6
6544  004054  LWAB:     B,R2 ALUF,PASSB ALUD,NONE
6545  004054            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6546  004054            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
6547  004055            A,R2 B,R7 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
6548  004055            IDBS,GPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6549  004055            LWABC CONDENABL;
6550  004056            B,R7 ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
6551  004056            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6552  004057  LWABC:    A,R7 B,1 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,NONE
6553  004057            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6554  004057            COND,F=0 F,JMP F,HOLD;
6555  004060            A,R7 B,R4 MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSA ALUD,SRB
6556  004060            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6557  004060            LWABB CONDENABL;
6558  004061            A,R6 B,R4 ALUF,A+B ALUD,Q
6559  004061            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6560  004062            B,R6 ALUF,Q-1 ALUD,B
6561  004062            IDBS,ALU T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,FAST;
6562  004063  LWABB:    A,R4 B,R4 ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B,YA
6563  004063            IDBS,ALU COMM,LDGPR T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6564  004064            A,R6 B,R6 ALUF,D+A ALUD,B
6565  004064            IDBS,GPR T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,FAST;
6566  004065  %********************************************************************
6567  004065  % SUBROUTINE TO COMPUTE NUMBER OF WORDS
6568  004065  % AND BOTTOM WORD ADDRESS OF A BCD-OPERAND
6569  004065  % DESCRIPTORS IN R1,R2
6570  004065  % RETURN WITH NUMBER OF WORDS IN STS
6571  004065  % NUMBER OF NIBLES IN R3
6572  004065  % BOTTOM WORD ADDRESS IN R1
6573  004065  LWA:      A,R2 B,1 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,NONE
6574  004065            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6575  004065            COND,F=0 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
6576  004066            A,R2 B,STS ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,B
6577  004066            IDBS,GPR T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6578  004066            LWAA CONDENABL;
6579  004067            B,STS ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
6580  004067            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6581  004070  LWAA:     A,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
6582  004070            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6583  004070            COND,F15 F,JMP F,HOLD;
6584  004071            B,STS MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSB ALUD,SRB
6585  004071            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6586  004071            LWAC CONDENABL;
6587  004072            B,STS ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
6588  004072            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6589  004073  LWAC:     A,STS B,R3 MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSA ALUD,SLB
6590  004073            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6591  004074            B,STS MIS,ZIN ALUF,PASSB ALUD,SRB
6592  004074            IDBS,ALU T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST;
6593  004075            A,R3 B,2 ALUF,ANDDA ALUD,NONE
6594  004075            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6595  004075            COND,F=0 F,NEXT;
6596  004076            A,STS B,R1 ALUF,A+B ALUD,B
6597  004076            IDBS,ALU T,JMP T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6598  004076            LWAD CONDENABL;
6599  004077            B,STS ALUF,B+1 ALUD,B
6600  004077            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDGPR T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,FAST
6601  004077            37;
6602  004100  LWAD:     B,R1 ALUF,B-1 ALUD,B
6603  004100            IDBS,ARG COMM,LDGPR T,RETURN T,POP CYCL,FAST
6604  004100            37;
6605  004101  %********************************************************************
6606  004101  % SUBROUTINE TO MAKE MASK FOR TOPWORD BCD-OPERANDS
6607  004101  % DESCRIPTOR IN R2. RETURN WITH MASK IN Z.
6608  004101  TTPWD:    A,R2 ALUF,PASSA ALUD,NONE
6609  004101            IDBS,GPR COMM,LDLC T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6610  004101            COND,F15 F,NEXT F,HOLD;
6611  004102            ALUD,NONE

6613  004102            RIGTB CONDENABL;

6615  004103            IDBS,BARG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6616  004103            COND,F=0 F,RETURN F,POP;
6617  004104            A,14 B,Z ALUF,D-1 ALUD,B
6618  004104            IDBS,BMG T,NEXT T,HOLD CYCL,FAST
6619  004104            CONDENABL;

9952  006350  %********************************************************************
9953  006350  %********************************************************************
9954  006350  % INSTRUCTIONS TO SPEED UP SINTRAN III SEGMENT HANDLING
9955  006350  % THE INSTRUCTION GROUP 'S3SEG'   OPCODE 1403XX
9956  006350  %********************************************************************
9957  006350  %********************************************************************
9958  006350  % OPCODE 140300   :   SETPT
9959  006350  % SETPT:   JXZ * 10   % FINISHED
9960  006350  % LDDTX 20
9961  006350  % BSET ZRO 130 DA % PGU-BIT
9962  006350  % LDBTX 10
9963  006350  % 177777   % OLD BUG IN LDBTX
9964  006350  % STD ,B   % ALWAYS INSIDE PAGE TABLE
9965  006350  % LDXTX 00
9966  006350  % JMP *-7
9967  006350  %********************************************************************
9968  006350  % OPCODE 140301   :   CLEPT
9969  006350  % CLEPT:   JXZ * 11   % FINISHED
9970  006350  % LDBTX 10
9971  006350  % 177777   % OLD BUG IN LDBTX
9972  006350  % LDA ,B
9973  006350  % JAZ * 3
9974  006350  % STATX 20
9975  006350  % STZ ,B   % ALWAYS INSIDE PAGE TABLE
9976  006350  % LDXTX 00
9977  006350  % JMP *-10
9978  006350  %********************************************************************
9979  006350  % OPCODE 140302   :   CLNREENT
9980  006350  % READ ADDRESS  A+2 TO FIND PAGE TABLE TO BE AFFECTED.
9981  006350  % READ RT-DESCRIPTION BITMAP WORDS, FOUND FROM ADDRESS X+25.
9982  006350  % CLEAR PAGE-TABLE ENTRIES CORRESPONDING TO 1-BITS IN BITMAP,
9983  006350  % THE LAST BITMAP-WORD IS IN ADDRESS X+T.
9984  006350  %********************************************************************
9985  006350  % OPCODE 140303   :   CHREENTPAGES
9986  006350  % 1. READ ADDRESS  D,X -> R1 ;  D,X -> PREVIOUS (SCRATCH REG)
9987  006350  % 2. IF R1 = 0; SKIP RETURN (FINISHED)
9988  006350  % 3. READ ADDRESS T,R1+2
9989  006350  % 4. IF NOT WIP; T,R1 -> PREVIOUS; READ ADDR T,R1  ->  R1; GOTO 2
9990  006350  % 5. READ ADDRESS T,R1 -> R2
9991  006350  % 6. WRITE R2 -> ADDRESS PREVIOUS
9992  006350  % 7. R1 -> X ; PREVIOUS -> D,A ; RETURN
9993  006350  %********************************************************************
9994  006350  % OPCODE 140304   :   CLEPU
9995  006350  % AS 'CLEPT', BUT INCLUDING WORKING SET INFORMATION
9996  006350  % FOR ALL PAGE-TABLE ENTRIES HANDLED
9997  006350  % IF PGU OF ENTRY IS 1
9998  006350  % D /\ 300
9999  006350  % B /\ 776 SHR 1 - D
10000  006350  % B-REG BITS 0-3 IS NOW BIT NUMBER
10001  006350  % B-REG BITS 4-6 IS NOW WORD NUMBER
10002  006350  % SET BIT IN 8-WORD TABLE IN PAGE-MAP BANK
10003  006350  % POINTED TO BY L-REGISTER
10004  006350  % LAYOUT OF 8-WORD TABLE :
10005  006350  % BIT 15                                          BIT 0
10006  006350  % ---------------------------------------------
10007  006350  % L-REG -> WORD 0 # PAGE 17                              PAGE 0 #
10008  006350  % WORD 1 # PAGE 37                                     20 #
10009  006350  % WORD 2 # PAGE 57                                     40 #
10010  006350  % WORD 7 # PAGE 177                                   160 #
10011  006350  % ---------------------------------------------
10012  006350  %********************************************************************
10013  006350  %********************************************************************
10014  006350  S3SEG:    A,A ALUF,A+1
10015  006350            IDBS,DBR COMM,LDGPR T,JMP0-3 T,HOLD CYCL,WAIT
10016  006350            SETPT;
